## CFIR DCE unused diagnostics - suppress CHIR DCE warnings when a file has errors

- problem type: CFIR must not report CHIR-DCE-style `UNUSED_VARIABLE` / `UNUSED_EXPRESSION` warnings for a file that already has errors, because official `cjc` only runs CHIR DCE reporting after sema succeeds.
- root cause: `CfirUnusedExpressionChecker` reports DCE-like warnings during the ordinary front-end checker walk, so warnings were committed before later declaration errors in the same file, causing many Extend fixtures with real errors to gain official-invalid `UNUSED_VARIABLE` markers.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir_unused_variable_probe.cj` reported `chir_dce_unused_variable` for unused local `let`/`var` when the file had no errors; after adding `errorProbe(): Int64 { let unusedInErrorFunction = 1; return "not Int64" }`, the same command reported only `sema_mismatched_types` and `Warnings: 0`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/CHIR/Transformation/DeadCodeElimination.cpp` runs `ReportUnusedCode`, `ReportUnusedCodeInFunc`, and `ReportUnusedLocalVariable` in CHIR DCE, after sema.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/extra/UnusedVariableAssignmentChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirUnusedCheckerBase.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/extra/FirUnusedExpressionChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `common/diagnostics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/diagnostics/impl/PendingDiagnosticsReporterImpl.kt`.
- repair principle: delay CFIR DCE warnings in the shared pending diagnostic reporter until end-of-file and discard them when the same file has any error, preserving official CHIR phase ordering without fixture-specific suppression.
- fixtures covered: the regression covers the previously re-failing Extend entries where `UNUSED_VARIABLE` was the only new pollution, including `testInvariantOverrideReturntype01`, `testInvariantOverrideReturntype02`, `testExtendFunctionOverride3`, `testExtendFunctionOverride4`, and `testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous1` through `testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous4` in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: failed only on remaining Extend families; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=57 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=58 errors=0`, with `UNUSED_VARIABLE` failure count reduced to `8` in each suite and no failures for `testInvariantOverrideReturntype01`, `testInvariantOverrideReturntype02`, or `testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous1` through `testExtendInstantiationFunctionAmbiguous4`.

## CFIR unused-variable usage - function values are read when invoked

- problem type: a local variable holding a function value must be considered used when it is invoked through a direct `CfirFunctionCall`, such as `funcPtr(5)`.
- root cause: `CfirUnusedExpressionChecker` collected reads from `CfirQualifiedAccessExpression`, but generated visitor dispatch visits `CfirFunctionCall` through `visitFunctionCall`, so function-valued local variables used as callees were not inserted into the used-variable set.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\extend_func_ptr.cj` produced no diagnostics. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/CHIR/Transformation/DeadCodeElimination.cpp` determines local unused variables from CHIR operand users, and a function-value call creates a real use.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/extra/UnusedVariableAssignmentChecker.kt`, where variable accesses in the control-flow graph mark visible writes as used.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirUnusedExpressionChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the fix in the shared unused-variable usage collector by treating `CfirFunctionCall.calleeReference` the same way as ordinary qualified access references, so any function-valued local call is counted as a read.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuncPtr`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuncPtr`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuncPtr' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuncPtr' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI `testExtendFuncPtr` passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `328 tests completed, 111 failed`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=55 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=56 errors=0`, and `testExtendFuncPtr` no longer appeared in either failure list.

## CFIR unused-variable DCE - local binding initialized from `this` is not warned

- problem type: local bindings whose initializer is the current `this` receiver must not produce `UNUSED_VARIABLE`, while ordinary unused locals in the same member body still do.
- root cause: CFIR modeled unused local variables only from source-level read tracking and did not account for the official CHIR DCE shape where `this` receiver initializers do not produce the debug/use pattern that reports an unused local variable.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\extend_this4.cj` produced no diagnostics. `tmp/cfir_unused_this_initializer_probe.cj` reported `chir_dce_unused_variable` only for `unusedLiteralValue`, not for `unusedThisValue: ThisOwner = this`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/CHIR/AST2CHIR/TranslateASTNode/TranslateVarDecl.cpp` and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/CHIR/Transformation/DeadCodeElimination.cpp` show that unused-variable warnings are driven by CHIR debug/use nodes rather than source declarations alone.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/extra/UnusedVariableAssignmentChecker.kt`, used only as the framework counterpart for read/write based unused-variable analysis.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirUnusedExpressionChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the official CHIR-DCE exception in the shared local-binding collection path by excluding bindings initialized from `CfirThisReceiverExpression`, while preserving normal unused-variable detection for other initializers.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendThis4`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendThis4`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendThis4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendThis4' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI `testExtendThis4` passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `328 tests completed, 109 failed`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=54 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=55 errors=0`, and `testExtendThis4` no longer appeared in either failure list.

## Match type-pattern usefulness - open type intersections stay reachable

- problem type: `case value: PatternType` must stay reachable when the selector type and pattern type can have an overlapping runtime subtype, even if their static CFIR types are not equal.
- root cause: CFIR's Maranget row specialization treated a `CfirMatchPatternKind.Type` row as matching only when `patternType == selectorColumnType`, so `Collection<Int64>` matched against `Array<Int64>` was rejected before usefulness could see that `Array<Int64>` is a possible runtime subtype. The same model also lacked the official pre-check that only `Nothing` or sealed-like disjoint type patterns are statically unreachable.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\unbox_array.cj` produced no diagnostics. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PatternUsefulness.cpp` `FromTypePattern` converts `goalTy <: patternTy` into wildcard, `IsUnreachableTypePattern` keeps generic and non-sealed-like type patterns reachable, and `Constructor::IsCoveredBy` uses `candidateTy <: patternTy` for TYPE constructors. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckPattern.cpp` `ChkTypePattern` marks non-subtype type patterns as runtime checks instead of rejecting every non-equal type pair.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/psi2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/builder/PsiConversionUtils.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/light-tree2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/lightTree/converter/LightTreeRawFirExpressionBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`. Kotlin builds subject `is` conditions as `FirTypeOperatorCall(IS)` and resolves them through the shared type-operator/data-flow path instead of rejecting them through static type equality.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/match/exhaustive/inria/MarangetChecker.kt`, `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/match/exhaustive/inria/RowSpecializer.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/match/exhaustive/inria/MarangetChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/match/exhaustive/inria/RowSpecializer.kt`.
- repair principle: keep reachability in the shared Maranget usefulness model by making TYPE constructor specialization use subtype coverage and by applying the official sealed-like disjoint pre-check, so PSI and light-tree diagnostics share the same open-type runtime-match semantics.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnboxArray`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnboxArray`; related `testExtendInstantiate4` in both suites no longer reports `UNREACHABLE_PATTERN` for `case v: Array<T>`, but that method remains red for the separate `UNUSED_VARIABLE` warning on `main`'s local `a`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnboxArray' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnboxArray' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: the command exceeded the tool timeout while Gradle was still running, then completed; XML showed both PSI and non-PSI `testUnboxArray` with `tests=1 failures=0 errors=0`.
- related verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnbox*' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnbox*' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiate4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiate4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testBoxTuplePattern' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testBoxTuplePattern' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- related verification outcome: failed with `24 tests completed, 14 failed`; XML showed `testUnboxArray`, `testAutoboxMatch1`, `testAutoboxMatch2`, `testAutoboxMatch3`, and `testBoxTuplePattern` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites. The remaining failures are separate enum/tuple/option pattern coverage, unresolved or-pattern binding, imported-interface extend, and `UNUSED_VARIABLE` diagnostics rather than the open type-pattern reachability fixed here.

## Inherited member type consistency - static/non-static conflicts suppress type inconsistency

- problem type: a same-name function group that already has a static/non-static conflict must not additionally report `INHERIT_MEMBER_TYPE_INCONSISTENT` from the use-site inherited function set.
- root cause: `CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.checkInheritedMemberTypeConsistency` collected extension functions from the target type's use-site scope as inherited functions and compared their return types by override signature. For `extend A` with both static and non-static `me`, this produced a class-level `INHERIT_MEMBER_TYPE_INCONSISTENT` after the correct `STATIC_FUNCTION_OVERLOAD_CONFLICTS` diagnostics had already been reported.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir_extend_conflict_interface_probe.cj` reported only two `sema_static_function_overload_conflicts` diagnostics on the static `me` declarations, with no inherited member type inconsistency. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` `PreCheckFuncStaticConflict` reports static declarations against the first non-static same-name function. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` `DiagnoseForConflictInheritance` erases a member name after kind conflict and explicitly avoids further inherited type inconsistency for that name.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirConflictsDeclarationChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/FirConflictsHelpers.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep suppression in the shared inherited-member type consistency checker by detecting mixed static/non-static same-name function groups before return-type comparison, so PSI and light-tree paths both respect the official diagnostic ordering instead of fixture-local filtering.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendConflictInterface`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendConflictInterface`; related `testStaticNonstaticFuncConflict` stayed green in both suites.

## Extend duplicate interfaces - instantiated targets and primitive built-in interfaces

- problem type: extend duplicate-interface diagnostics must compare instantiated target interfaces, constructor instantiation extend interfaces, and primitive built-in interfaces against the new extend super interface list.
- root cause: CFIR duplicate-interface checks compared only the current extend's local super interface keys and other extend declarations for the same target. They did not substitute a generic target declaration before comparing its inherited interfaces, did not include constructor-call instantiation checks against applicable extend-provided interfaces, used the declaration-name positioning strategy for instantiation diagnostics, and treated a primitive built-in `ToString` duplicate as an imported-interface error instead of `EXTEND_DUPLICATE_INTERFACE`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all` on `extend_duplicate_interfaces6.cj` reported `sema_extend_duplicate_interface` on `extend<T> B<T> <: I<T>` and `sema_inherit_duplicate_interface` at `B<String>()`; `extend_duplicate_interfaces8.cj` produced no diagnostics; `extend_duplicate_interfaces9.cj` reported `sema_extend_duplicate_interface` on `ToString`; `extend_duplicate_interfaces10.cj` reported `sema_inherit_duplicate_interface` at `B<String, String>()`. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp` `GetDupInterfaceRecursively`, `GetExtendDupSuperInterface`, `GetDupSuperInterface`, and `CheckInstDupSuperInterfaces`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExtend.cpp` extend duplicate/specialization/imported-interface checks, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeManager.cpp` `IsSubtype` / `GetAllExtendInterfaceTy`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirSupertypesChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/cfir-common/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/session/services/CfirExtendRuleQueryService.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirExtendSemanticModel.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirExtendRuleQueryService.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirExtendTypeSemanticNormalizer.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirExtendIndexStore.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirDuplicateSuperInterfaceUtils.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirGenericInstantiationChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirExtendCheckers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirExtendExtraChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CfirExtendSemantics.kt`.
- repair principle: keep all duplicate-interface decisions in the shared extend semantics index and duplicate-super-interface checker path, so declaration checks and instantiation checks consume the same substituted interface semantics instead of fixture-specific diagnostics.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendDuplicateInterfaces6`, `testExtendDuplicateInterfaces8`, `testExtendDuplicateInterfaces9`, `testExtendDuplicateInterfaces10`, and the same four methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendDuplicateInterfaces6' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendDuplicateInterfaces6' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendDuplicateInterfaces8' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendDuplicateInterfaces8' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendDuplicateInterfaces9' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendDuplicateInterfaces9' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendDuplicateInterfaces10' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendDuplicateInterfaces10' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all eight focused PSI and non-PSI methods passed.

## Extend duplicate interfaces - target superclass interface closure

- problem type: extend duplicate-interface diagnostics must treat interfaces inherited through the target class's superclass chain as already implemented by the extend target.
- root cause: CFIR's target-interface comparison only collected direct interface nodes. For `class B <: A` and `open class A <: Foo`, the collector stopped when it saw `A` because it was a class, so `extend B <: Foo` did not compare against the inherited `Foo` interface. The checker fallback had the same shape: it only recursed into resolved `CfirInterface` declarations and therefore skipped interfaces reachable through class-like supertypes.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cfir_extend_duplicate_interfaces3_probe.cj` reported `sema_extend_duplicate_interface` on `Foo` in `extend B <: Foo`, with message `interface 'Interface-Foo' has been implemented by 'Class-B', please remove it`. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp` `GetDupInterfaceRecursively` and `GetDupSuperInterface`, where each inherited type is instantiated and then recursively inspected; class-like declarations are tracked in `passedClassLikeDecls`, and interface collection continues through the superclass path instead of stopping at a class node.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirSupertypesChecker.kt`, used for checker placement and source-based supertype iteration architecture.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirExtendIndexStore.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirExtendCheckers.kt`.

## Unresolved member access diagnostics - primitive receivers are non-nominal

- problem type: unresolved member access on primitive receivers must report `UNRESOLVED_REFERENCE`, while `NOT_MEMBER_OF` remains reserved for class/interface, struct, and enum receivers.
- root cause: `ConeUnresolvedNameError.mapNotMemberOfDiagnostic` checked `receiver.classIdOrPrimitiveClassId`, so `ConePrimitiveType` was treated like a nominal member receiver and mapped to `NOT_MEMBER_OF`. Official Cangjie only reports `sema_not_member_of` when `baseExpr->ty->IsNominal()` is true; primitive member lookup failure falls through to `sema_undeclared_identifier`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cfir_extend_primitive_type_02_probe.cj` reported `sema_undeclared_identifier` on `addd` in `1.addd`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp` `DiagMemberAccessNotFound` reports `sema_not_member_of` only for `baseExpr->ty->IsNominal()` and otherwise reports `sema_undeclared_identifier`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/AST/Types.cpp` `Ty::IsNominal` is exactly class/interface/struct/enum.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`, where `ConeUnresolvedNameError` maps through the unresolved-reference diagnostic path rather than a broad receiver-type `NOT_MEMBER_OF` fallback.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the fix in the shared cone-diagnostic mapper and align its receiver classification with official nominal-type semantics, so every unresolved member access uses the same class/interface/struct/enum boundary.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendPrimitiveType02`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendPrimitiveType02`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendPrimitiveType02' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendPrimitiveType02' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; both PSI and non-PSI `testExtendPrimitiveType02` passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: failed with `328 tests completed, 93 failed`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=46 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=47 errors=0`, and `testExtendPrimitiveType02` had no failure node in either suite.

## Bare generic static qualifier call inference - owner and extend parameters stay inferable

- problem type: bare generic class qualifier static member and static extend-member calls must keep owner and extend type parameters inferable from call arguments instead of treating the qualifier's default self type as explicit type arguments.
- root cause: CFIR represented the bare classifier qualifier `A` as the default self type `A<T>` and then interpreted that type as an explicit use-site substitution / non-bare receiver. This fixed class owner `T` too early for `A.ad(s)` and filtered the static extend candidate for `A.add(s)` before the call constraint system could infer `T = S`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\extend_generic_infer.cj` produced no semantic errors and only `chir_dce_unused_variable` warnings for the unused parameters in `ad` and `add`. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/ExtraScopes.cpp` `IsBaseTypeOmittedTypeArgs`, `InstCtxScope::GenerateTypeMappingByCallContext`, `InstCtxScope::GenerateExtendGenericTypeMapping`, `TypeCheckerImpl::GenerateTypeMappingForBaseExpr`, and `InstCtxScope::SetRefDecl`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeArgumentInference.cpp` `PrepareTyArgsSynthesis`, `GenerateTypeMappingForCall`, and `GenerateTypeMappingByInference`; and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` `FilterTargetsInExtend` / `FilterAndCheckTargetsOfNameAccess`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CreateFreshTypeVariableSubstitutorStage.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/ResolutionStages.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/candidate/Candidate.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/CfirCallableOwnerUseSiteSubstitution.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassStaticScope.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirExtendMemberScope.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassUseSiteMemberScope.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCreateFreshTypeVariableSubstitutorStage.kt`.
- repair principle: treat the default self type emitted for a bare classifier qualifier as an inference carrier, not as explicit type arguments, so class owner and extend parameters enter the candidate constraint system fresh while enum constructors keep their existing explicit qualifier substitution behavior.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGenericInfer`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGenericInfer`; regression guard `testAutoboxMatch1` was rerun in both PSI and non-PSI suites to confirm enum-constructor inference was not disturbed.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGenericInfer' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGenericInfer' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: the first run timed out in the tool window, but Gradle completed naturally and XML showed both PSI and non-PSI `testExtendGenericInfer` with `tests=1 failures=0 errors=0`.
- guard command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGenericInfer' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGenericInfer' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch1' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## Match or-pattern bindings - illegal bindings report once and do not enter branch scope

- problem type: bindings introduced by `|`-connected patterns must report one `VAR_IN_OR_PATTERN` main diagnostic at the first binding occurrence, ordinary enum/var bindings in that illegal or-pattern must not enter the branch scope or same-branch redeclaration checks, and match exhaustiveness/reachability/pattern-legality follow-up checks must be skipped after the pattern is invalid.
- root cause: CFIR treated every binding inside an or-pattern as a visible local declaration in resolve and local-redeclaration visitors, reported `VAR_IN_OR_PATTERN` on every alternative, kept running `DIFFERENT_OR_PATTERN` and pattern-shape checks after a binding error, and flattened illegal or-pattern alternatives into the Maranget model.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cfir_or_pattern_binding_probe.cj` reported `sema_var_in_or_pattern` once on the first enum/var or type-pattern binding with later bindings as hints, and body references to ordinary enum/var bindings as `sema_undeclared_identifier`; `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cfir_unbox_multiEnumPattern_probe.cj` reported `sema_redefinition` for duplicated type-pattern bindings plus one `sema_var_in_or_pattern` main diagnostic per illegal or-pattern. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckMatchExpr.cpp` `ChkMatchCasePatterns` short-circuits after `ChkNoVarPatternInOrPattern` fails and only runs PatternUsefulness when the match expression type check succeeds; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckPattern.cpp` `ChkVarPattern` still reports same-scope redefinition for type-pattern bindings.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/light-tree2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/lightTree/converter/LightTreeRawFirExpressionBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirWhenConditionChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirExhaustiveWhenChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/cfir-tree/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/patterns/CfirPatternBindingUtils.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirPatternBindingResolution.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConflictsDeclarationChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConflictsHelpers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirPatternExpressionChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirMatchPatternLegalityChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirMatchExhaustivenessChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirMatchUnreachablePatternChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/match/CfirMatchPatternModel.kt`, `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/match/CfirMatchPatternModel.kt`, `cfir/raw-cfir/psi2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/builder/PsiRawCfirBuilder.kt`, `cfir/raw-cfir/light-tree2cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/lightTree/LightTreeRawCfirExpressionBuilder.kt`.
- repair principle: keep illegal or-pattern binding behavior in shared pattern binding, checker, resolve, conflict, and match-model owners so PSI and light-tree paths both use the same official visibility and diagnostic-ordering semantics.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnboxMultiEnumPattern`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testUnboxMultiEnumPattern`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.enum.EnumGenerated.testEnum4`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.enum.EnumGenerated.testEnum4`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.PatternMatching.TypePattern.TypePatternGenerated.testType07`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.PatternMatching.TypePattern.TypePatternGenerated.testType07`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.PatternMatching.MatchExpression.MatchExpressionGenerated.testErrDifferentPattern00`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.PatternMatching.MatchExpression.MatchExpressionGenerated.testErrDifferentPattern00`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.PatternMatching.EnumPattern.EnumPatternGenerated.testEnum09`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.PatternMatching.EnumPattern.EnumPatternGenerated.testEnum09`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.PatternMatching.EnumPattern.EnumPatternGenerated.testEnum10`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.PatternMatching.EnumPattern.EnumPatternGenerated.testEnum10`.
- fixture corrections: `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/enum/enum4.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/PatternMatching/EnumPattern/enum09.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/PatternMatching/EnumPattern/enum10.cj`, and `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/PatternMatching/TypePattern/type07.cj` were updated to record the repository diagnostic surface for the official main diagnostic only; official later-binding hints are not represented as LLT inline diagnostics.

## Extend generic interface implementation - instantiated receiver resolves implementation over abstract interface member

- problem type: a generic extend that implements a generic interface method must let calls on an instantiated receiver choose the concrete extend implementation, not report ambiguity between the implementation and the inherited abstract interface member.
- root cause: `CfirClassUseSiteMemberScope` compared extend members against parent interface members with the target class owner substitutor. For `extend<T> A<T> <: I<T>`, that substitutor does not replace the extend declaration's own `T`, so `test(T)` from the extend and substituted `test(Int32)` from `I<Int32>` were not recognized as the same override signature.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\extend_instantiate2.cj` produced no diagnostics. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/ExtraScopes.cpp` `GenerateExtendGenericTypeMapping` and `SetRefDecl` derive extend generic mappings from the call receiver; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/GenericInstantiation/GenericInstantiationContext.cpp` maps interface functions to implementation functions after substituting the target/interface type relationship.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/overloads/ConeOverloadConflictResolver.kt`, where overload conflict resolution first filters candidates by override partial order; `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/overloads/ConeEquivalentCallConflictResolver.kt`, where equivalent callable candidates are compared through the standard override checker after substitution.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassUseSiteMemberScope.kt`.
- repair principle: keep extend-owner substitution in the shared use-site scope override-signature comparison, so call resolution, inherited-member filtering, and direct-overridden chains all see the same instantiated extend implementation relationship.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiate2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiate2`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiate2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiate2' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: the command exceeded the tool timeout while Gradle was still running, then completed; XML showed both PSI and non-PSI `testExtendInstantiate2` with `tests=1 failures=0 errors=0`.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: failed with `328 tests completed, 87 failed`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=43 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=44 errors=0`, and `testExtendInstantiate2` had no failure node in either suite.

## Extend instantiate unused-local fixture correction - error-free file keeps local DCE warning

- problem type: `extend_instantiate4.cj` is an error-free file, so the LLT fixture must keep the repository `UNUSED_VARIABLE` diagnostic for the unused local variable in `main`.
- root cause: the fixture omitted the local unused-variable warning even though CFIR's shared unused-local checker and official CHIR DCE both report unused locals in error-free files.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\extend_instantiate4.cj` reported two `chir_dce_unused_variable` warnings, one for the constructor parameter `a` at line 4 and one for the local `var a: A<Int64>` at line 20. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/CHIR/Transformation/DeadCodeElimination.cpp` reports unused locals through `ReportUnusedCode`, `ReportUnusedCodeInFunc`, and `ReportUnusedLocalVariable`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/extra/UnusedVariableAssignmentChecker.kt`, used as the framework counterpart for read/write based unused-variable analysis.
- CFIR owner files changed: none; this entry is a fixture correction backed by official behavior and the existing repository diagnostic surface.
- repair principle: keep the correction in the fixture because the shared CFIR owner already reports the officially valid local unused-variable warning in both PSI and light-tree paths.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiate4`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiate4`.
- fixture correction: `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_instantiate4.cj` now marks `main`'s `var a` with `UNUSED_VARIABLE`; the official constructor-parameter warning is not added because the current LLT diagnostic surface for `CfirUnusedExpressionChecker` scans function-body local pattern variables, not constructor parameters.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiate4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInstantiate4' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## Extend supertype covariance - implicit Object superclass participates in return override subtype checks

- problem type: class return types in extend/interface implementations must be valid covariant overrides of `Object` return requirements through the implicit `Object` superclass.
- root cause: CFIR's type-aware direct supertype provider returned only declared supertypes for classes. Classes without an explicit concrete superclass therefore did not participate in the official implicit `Object` superclass graph, so override return subtype checks rejected `Obj` as an implementation return for `Object`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\extend_supertype1.cj` produced no diagnostics. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` `TypeCheckerImpl::AddSuperClassObjectForClassDecl` adds `Object` as superclass for class declarations without an explicit superclass, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckUtil.cpp` `CheckThisTypeCompatibility` / `AreReturnTypesCompatible` allow child return types that are subtypes of the parent return type.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirStandardOverrideChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirTypeAwareSupertypeProviderImpl.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_supertype6.cj`.

## Extend abstract-class target member implementation - abstract class signatures satisfy extend interface requirements

- problem type: `extend C <: I` must not require a new extend-body implementation when abstract class `C` already declares an abstract member with the interface requirement signature.
- root cause: `CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.checkExtendTargetMemberCompatibility` treated only non-abstract target members as satisfying interface requirements. That incorrectly made an extend on an abstract class report `NEED_MEMBER_IMPLEMENTATION` even though the official checker skips abstract members inherited from abstract classes for extend declarations.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\extend_supertype9.cj` produced no diagnostics. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` `DiagnoseForUnimplementedInterfaces` skips `extendInheritedAbstract` when the member is abstract and its owner is abstract while checking an extend declaration, and `CheckImplementationRelation` separately validates matching function signatures.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirNotImplementedOverrideChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the distinction in the shared extend-target implementation checker: concrete target members satisfy ordinary interface obligations, while abstract members owned by abstract classes satisfy the extend declaration's signature obligation without erasing real missing-implementation diagnostics.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSupertype9`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSupertype9`; guard fixtures `testAbstrctFuncUnimplement` and `testExtendInterface3` stayed red/green as expected in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSupertype9' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSupertype9' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAbstrctFuncUnimplement' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAbstrctFuncUnimplement' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface3' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testExtendSupertype9` and both guard fixtures passed in PSI and non-PSI suites.
- full supertype-family command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSupertype*' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSupertype*' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- full supertype-family outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; `testExtendSupertype1` through `testExtendSupertype9` passed in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## Extend interface default implementation and invalid-interface cascade suppression

- problem type: `extend T <: A & B` must let a default member inherited from one interface satisfy a same-signature abstract member from another interface, while invalid extend supertypes must not produce secondary `EXTEND_NOT_INTERFACE` or `EXTEND_DUPLICATE_INTERFACE` cascade diagnostics after the primary type error.
- root cause: `CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.checkExtendTargetMemberCompatibility` only considered target members, other visible extends, and own extend-body members as implementation candidates, so a sibling default interface member in the same extend declaration could not satisfy an abstract requirement. `CfirExtendInterfaceKindChecker` also treated `CfirErrorTypeRef` as definitely non-interface, and `CfirExtendDuplicateInterfaceChecker` ran duplicate-interface logic on resolved non-interface supertypes.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\extend_interface4.cj` produced no diagnostics; `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cjc-extend-interface\extend_interface6_clean.cj` reported only `sema_generic_type_without_type_argument` for `Foo`; `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cjc-extend-interface\extend_interface7_clean.cj` reported only `sema_extend_not_interface` on the non-interface supertypes, with no duplicate-interface diagnostic. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExtend.cpp` `CheckExtendInterfaces` skips non-interface instantiated supertypes before duplicate-interface checks, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` `DiagnoseForUnimplementedInterfaces` checks the merged member table rather than each interface in isolation.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirNotImplementedOverrideChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/FirHelpers.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirExtendCheckers.kt`.
- repair principle: keep default-interface implementation and invalid-interface cascade suppression in the shared extend inheritance/checker owners so PSI and light-tree paths both use the same merged-interface semantics and do not patch individual fixtures.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface4`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface4`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface6`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface6`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface7`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface7`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface6' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface6' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface7' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface7' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed PSI and non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` each ran `tests=3 failures=0 errors=0`.

## Extend interface nested type-argument error cascade

- problem type: an extend supertype whose classifier resolves but whose type argument contains an unresolved type must still report `EXTEND_NOT_INTERFACE` for the invalid instantiated supertype, while missing or mismatched generic argument counts must stop after their primary generic diagnostic.
- root cause: the earlier invalid-interface cascade suppression treated every `CfirErrorTypeRef` as undecidable for extend interface kind checks. That correctly suppressed `EXTEND_NOT_INTERFACE` after `ConeUnmatchedTypeArgumentsError`, but incorrectly suppressed the official `EXTEND_NOT_INTERFACE` cascade when `ConeUnreportedDuplicateDiagnostic` wraps an inner type-argument error and the instantiated interface type is invalid.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cjc-extend-interface\extend_fuzz1_clean.cj` reported both `sema_undeclared_type_name` on `Poit` and `sema_extend_not_interface` on `Add<Point, Poit>`; `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cjc-extend-interface\extend_interface6_clean.cj` reported only `sema_generic_type_without_type_argument` for bare `Foo`; `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cjc-extend-interface\extend_interface_arg_mismatch_clean.cj` reported only `sema_generic_argument_no_match`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExtend.cpp` `PreCheckExtend` reports `sema_extend_not_interface` when inherited type `Ty` is not correct or not `TYPE_INTERFACE`, while `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckType.cpp` keeps generic argument-count errors as primary type diagnostics.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/collectors/AbstractDiagnosticCollectorVisitor.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/tree/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/Utils.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirExtendCheckers.kt`.
- repair principle: classify extend supertype error types by structured `ConeDiagnostic` kind in the shared extend checker, so generic argument-count errors do not cascade but nested type-argument errors still invalidate the instantiated interface in both PSI and light-tree paths.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuzz1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuzz1`; guard fixtures `testExtendInterface6` and `testExtendInterface7` stayed passing in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuzz1' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; non-PSI `testExtendFuzz1` passed.
- guard command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFuzz1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface6' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface6' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface7' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface7' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## Extend member access control - package visibility and private export surface

- problem type: extend member lookup must distinguish inaccessible non-private members from non-exported private members across package boundaries, and protected/default package rules must match official package-relation semantics in both direct and inherited use-site scopes.
- root cause: CFIR treated protected members as visible across the same module without applying official package relation rules, kept private extend members in cross-package use-site scopes, and cached extend member scopes without the use-site package. `CfirCompositeExtendProvider` also failed to forward extend package ownership, so private filtering could not determine whether an extend member belonged to the current package.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all` probes showed `extend_access_control1` external `a.test()` and `extend_access_control2` extend-body call to class-private `goo()` both report `sema_no_match_function_declaration_for_call`; split-package `protect_other_pkg` reports `sema_no_match_function_declaration_for_call` for protected function `f` and `sema_invalid_access_control` for protected property `p`; split-package `default_other_pkg_child_class` reports `sema_no_match_function_declaration_for_call` for default function `f`, `sema_undeclared_identifier` for private `g`/`q`, and `sema_invalid_access_control` for default property `p`; same-package private extend members remain visible enough to produce no-match / invalid-access diagnostics; cross-package explicit private extend member access reports `sema_not_member_of`. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckUtil.cpp` `IsLegalAccess` / `SearchTargetDeclForProtectMember`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckAccess.cpp`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Modules/ModulesUtils.cpp` `PackageRelation`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/VisibilityUtils.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/FirVisibilityChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/FirKotlinScopeProvider.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirClassUseSiteMemberScope.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/VisibilityUtils.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/CfirVisibilitySemantics.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/CfirExtendProvider.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/CfirSessionExtendProvider.kt`, `analysis/low-level-api-cfir/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/analysis/low/level/api/cfir/providers/LLCfirSessionExtendProvider.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/CfirCompositeExtendProvider.kt`, `cfir/cfir-serialization/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/serialization/provider/CfirDeserializedExtendProvider.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/CfirAccessibilityFileScope.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/SupertypeUtils.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/CfirCangJieScopeProvider.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassStaticScope.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassUseSiteMemberScope.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirExtendMemberScope.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirExtendAccessibilityChecker.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/types/ConeInferenceContext.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckVisibility.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, and generated diagnostic surfaces for `NO_MATCH_FUNCTION_DECLARATION_FOR_CALL`.
- repair principle: put package-relation visibility, extend package ownership, and private extend export filtering in shared provider/scope/visibility owners so direct, inherited, static, source, library, PSI, and light-tree paths all use the same official access-control semantics.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendAccessControl1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendAccessControl1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendAccessControl2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendAccessControl2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.visibility.protect_other_pkg.ProtectOtherPkgGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.visibility.protect_other_pkg.ProtectOtherPkgGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.visibility.protect_other_pkg_child_class.ProtectOtherPkgChildClassGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.visibility.protect_other_pkg_child_class.ProtectOtherPkgChildClassGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.visibility.default_other_pkg_child_class.DefaultOtherPkgChildClassGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.visibility.default_other_pkg_child_class.DefaultOtherPkgChildClassGenerated`.

## Extend inheritance-cycle diagnostics and invalid extend-edge cascade suppression

- problem type: inheritance cycles that enter through an `extend` inherited-interface edge must report `INHERITANCE_CYCLE` on the extend declaration, direct declaration/typealias cycles must keep their own source, and illegal extend targets or non-interface inherited types must not produce secondary inheritance-cycle diagnostics.
- root cause: CFIR mapped all loop-in-supertype errors to `SUPER_TYPES_SELF_REFERENCE`, selected the raw type-ref source for multi-node declaration cycles, and recorded every resolved extend supertype into `CfirSuperTypeGraphStore` even when the official precheck would remove the invalid extend edge before inheritance-cycle DFS.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all` probes showed `extend_inheritance_circle3` reports `sema_illegal_extended_type` on `extend A <: C` plus `sema_inheritance_cycle` on `interface A` with cycle `A->B->C->A`; `extend_inheritance_circle4` reports `sema_inheritance_cycle` on `extend D <: C` with cycle `C->A->B->C`; `class_inheritance_cycle_01` reports `sema_inheritance_cycle` on class `A` plus `sema_recursive_constructor_call`; `extend_inheritance_circle1`, `extend_inheritance_circle2`, and `extend_interface7` do not report inheritance-cycle cascades. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` `CheckInheritanceCycleWithExtend`, `CheckInheritanceCycleDFS`, and `CheckInheritanceCycleDFSHandleVisiting`, and through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExtend.cpp` `PreCheckExtend` / `UpdateExtendMap`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirSupertypesResolution.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/transformers/CfirSupertypesResolution.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirSuperTypeGraphStore.kt`, `cfir/cfir-tree/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/diagnostics/DiagnosticKind.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirDeclarationDiagnosticSources.kt`, generated diagnostic surfaces for `INHERITANCE_CYCLE`, and fixture corrections in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_inheritance_circle3.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_inheritance_circle4.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/class_inheritance_cycle_01.cj`.
- repair principle: keep cycle classification and extend-edge attribution in the shared SUPER_TYPES graph/loop-breaking pipeline, while filtering invalid extend graph edges at the single collector that feeds PSI and light-tree sessions.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInheritanceCircle1`, `testExtendInheritanceCircle2`, `testExtendInheritanceCircle3`, `testExtendInheritanceCircle4`, `testExtendInterface7`, the same five methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated`, plus guard fixtures `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassInheritanceCycle01` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassInheritanceCycle01`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface7' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInterface7' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInheritanceCircle1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInheritanceCircle1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInheritanceCircle2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInheritanceCircle2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInheritanceCircle3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInheritanceCircle3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInheritanceCircle4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInheritanceCircle4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassInheritanceCycle01' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassInheritanceCycle01' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## Extend unresolved-reference diagnostic ranges - full identifier tokens

- problem type: unresolved references in extend-related LLT fixtures must underline the full referenced identifier, not only the first character.
- root cause: the fixtures still used cjc-style one-character diagnostic anchors (`s`, `a`, `g`) even though this repository's Diagnostic Range Policy requires full relevant-token ranges for IDE diagnostics.
- official Cangjie evidence: not applicable; this is a range-only fixture correction governed by the Diagnostic Range Policy in `cangjie-cfir-llt-repair`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: not applicable; the current CFIR PSI and light-tree outputs already select the full identifier token.
- CFIR owner files changed: none.
- repair principle: correct the LLT expectations to the repository diagnostic range policy without changing semantic triggering or checker behavior.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGeneric5`, `testExtendNamelookup2`, `testExtendNamelookup8`, `testExtendNamelookup9`, and the same four methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated`.
- fixture corrections: `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_generic5.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_namelookup2.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_namelookup8.cj`, and `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_namelookup9.cj` now mark `soo`, `add`, and `go` as complete unresolved-reference tokens.
