## Extend target generic assumptions, invalid upper-bound cascades, and static generic dependency source placement

- problem type: extend target generic constraints must be projected onto extend type parameters, invalid upper-bound generic types must suppress secondary type-argument constraint cascades, and static variable/property generic-parameter dependency diagnostics must report on the dependent type or expression node.
- root cause: CFIR resolved an extend target such as `extend<R> A<R>` without exposing `A<T> where T <: C` as an assumption on `R`; the upper-bound checker still treated generic types with invalid upper bounds as eligible for later generic argument constraint checks; static generic dependency checking reported on the whole static field declaration and missed initializer/call expression nodes that depend on generic parameters.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir-probes/extend_generic10_probe.cj` reported `sema_upper_bound_must_be_class_or_interface`, `sema_mismatched_types` on `boo(a)`, and `sema_not_member_of`; the repository LLT diagnostic surface maps that argument mismatch to `ARGUMENT_TYPE_MISMATCH`. `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir-probes/extend_generic11_probe.cj` reported `sema_upper_bound_must_be_class_or_interface`, `sema_not_member_of`, and two `sema_static_variable_use_generic_parameter` diagnostics on the static field type `A<T>` and initializer `A<T>()`. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` `AddAssumptionForExtendDecls`, `AddUpperBoundOnTypeParameters`, `CreateGenericConstraints`, `ExposeGenericUpperBounds`, and `CheckAssumption`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckDecl.cpp` `GetRevTypeMapping`, `GetGenericTysToInstTysMapping`, and `GetAllAssumptions`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckGeneric.cpp` `CheckGenericDeclInstantiation`; and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/LegalityOfUsage/LegalityOfUsage.cpp` `CheckStaticMemberWithGeneric`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/type/FirUpperBoundViolatedTypeChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirUpperBoundViolatedQualifiedAccessExpressionChecker.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirConstPropertyChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/transformers/CfirTypeResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CfirUpperBoundViolatedHelpers.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirGeneralSemanticsChecker.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_generic10.cj`, and `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_generic11.cj`.
- repair principle: keep extend target assumption exposure in the shared type-resolution owner and keep upper-bound/static-generic diagnostics in shared checker helpers, so PSI and light-tree paths agree without fixture-local behavior.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMatchTypeArg`, `testExtendGenericConstraint`, `testExtendGeneric10`, `testExtendGeneric11`, `testExtendGeneric12`, and the same methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated`.
- fixture corrections: `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_generic10.cj` now expects the repository `ARGUMENT_TYPE_MISMATCH` diagnostic for the official `sema_mismatched_types` argument error; `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_generic11.cj` now records static generic dependency diagnostics on both the `A<T>` return type and the `A<T>()` initializer expression.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMatchTypeArg' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendMatchTypeArg' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGenericConstraint' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGenericConstraint' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGeneric10' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGeneric10' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGeneric11' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGeneric11' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGeneric12' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendGeneric12' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all focused PSI and non-PSI methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: failed with `328 tests completed, 32 failed`; XML showed the remaining failures were the same 16 methods in both PSI and non-PSI suites: `testUnboxBoxTuplePattern`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp1`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp5`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp6`, `testExtendFunctionConflict`, `testExtendInvalidType02`, `testUnboxEnumPattern`, `testExtendSubtype2`, `testExtendSubtype3`, `testUnboxAutoboxEnumPattern01`, `testUnboxAutoboxEnumPattern02`, `testUnboxOptionbox`, `testExtendStaticFuncAsVar`, `testExtendStaticFuncAsVarN`, `testAutoboxTryCatch1`, and `testExtendNamelookup7`.

## Extend constrained subtype conversion and callable-reference no-match diagnostics

- problem type: generic extend subtype conversion must expose constrained `A<T> <: B<T>` edges during inference, and a function declaration used as a function-typed argument must report the official callable-reference no-match diagnostic when its signature is not applicable under extend subtype rules.
- root cause: extend substitution filtering discarded `ConeTypeVariableType` while checking extend upper bounds, so inference could not keep `B<X>` available before fixation; after that was repaired, the function-reference path still collapsed incompatible declaration references into ordinary `ArgumentTypeMismatch`, and the no-match diagnostic did not carry the argument source, so it was anchored on the outer call.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir-probes/extend_subtype_probe.cj` reports `sema_mismatched_types` only for `A<Int64>` to `B<Int64>`, while accepting `A<Int32>` to `B<Int32>` and accepting `takesBInt32` as `(A<Int32>) -> Int64`. `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp/cfir-probes/extend_subtype3_callable_ref_probe.cj` reports `sema_no_match_function_declaration_for_ref` on `subFunc` at line 28 column 10, while `test2(subFunc2)` is accepted. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp`, where zero matched function-reference candidates emits `sema_no_match_function_declaration_for_ref`, and through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckerImpl.h` / `TypeCheckCall.cpp` argument type possibility handling.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/ResolutionStages.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CheckCallableReferenceExpectedType.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/semantics/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/ResolutionDiagnostic.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/CfirExtendSubstitution.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirExtendIndexStore.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirTypeAwareSupertypeProviderImpl.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/ConeResolutionAtom.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/ArgumentCheckingProcessor.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckCallableReferenceExpectedType.kt`, `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/diagnostic/ResolutionDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, generated diagnostic surfaces for `NO_MATCH_FUNCTION_DECLARATION_FOR_REF`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testFixtures/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/tests/golden/DiagnosticNameMapper.kt`, and `cfir/resolve/test/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/providers/CfirTypeAwareSupertypeProviderTest.kt`.
- repair principle: keep extend-derived generic subtype evidence in the shared extend substitution/supertype provider pipeline, then classify failed function-declaration references at the shared argument applicability and callable-reference diagnostic mapping layers instead of treating them as fixture-local type mismatches.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSubtype2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSubtype2`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSubtype3`, and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSubtype3`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSubtype2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSubtype2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSubtype3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendSubtype3' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all four focused PSI and non-PSI methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: failed with `328 tests completed, 28 failed`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=14 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=14 errors=0`. `testExtendSubtype2` and `testExtendSubtype3` had no failure nodes in either suite. Remaining failures in both suites are `testUnboxArray`, `testUnboxBoxTuplePattern`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp1`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp5`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp6`, `testExtendFunctionConflict`, `testExtendInvalidType02`, `testUnboxEnumPattern`, `testUnboxAutoboxEnumPattern01`, `testUnboxAutoboxEnumPattern02`, `testUnboxOptionbox`, `testExtendStaticFuncAsVarN`, `testAutoboxTryCatch1`, and `testExtendNamelookup7`.

## Extend qualified ambiguous-use diagnostic range

- problem type: non-call qualified ambiguous use must underline the complete qualified access such as `Int64.test`, while ambiguous function calls keep the callee/token source selected by the call diagnostic.
- root cause: `ConeAmbiguityError` was mapped from the selector source when the source was a custom-offset PSI source whose PSI element was narrower than its offsets, so rendering fell back to only `test` even though the error source represented `Int64.test`. The non-PSI collector also did not synthesize a qualified-access source from receiver start to reference end for ambiguity on error references.
- official Cangjie evidence: not applicable; this is a range-only repair governed by the Diagnostic Range Policy in `cangjie-cfir-llt-repair`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the range widening in the shared ambiguity diagnostic source mapping and error-reference collector, converting custom-offset PSI sources to offsets-only when the PSI element is narrower, while preserving call-like ambiguity source selection so function-call diagnostics do not become whole-qualified-access diagnostics.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendStaticFuncAsVarN`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendStaticFuncAsVarN`; guard fixtures `testExtendFunctionOverride3` and `testExtendFunctionOverride4` stayed passing in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendStaticFuncAsVarN' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendStaticFuncAsVarN' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFunctionOverride3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFunctionOverride3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFunctionOverride4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFunctionOverride4' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all six focused PSI and non-PSI methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: failed with `328 tests completed, 26 failed`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=13 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=13 errors=0`. `testExtendStaticFuncAsVarN`, `testExtendFunctionOverride3`, and `testExtendFunctionOverride4` had no failure nodes in either suite. Remaining failures in both suites are `testUnboxArray`, `testUnboxBoxTuplePattern`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp1`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp5`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp6`, `testExtendFunctionConflict`, `testExtendInvalidType02`, `testUnboxEnumPattern`, `testUnboxAutoboxEnumPattern01`, `testUnboxAutoboxEnumPattern02`, `testUnboxOptionbox`, `testAutoboxTryCatch1`, and `testExtendNamelookup7`.

## Extend primitive built-in operator overloads and interface implementation

- problem type: primitive built-in operators cannot be user-overloaded in extends, but the same built-in primitive operator signatures can satisfy abstract operator requirements inherited from interfaces.
- root cause: CFIR did not normalize primitive target/member operands through the shared built-in primitive operator model, so primitive operator declarations were not consistently classified for overload diagnostics, extend shadow checks, or inherited abstract-interface implementation matching. The subtype check for duplicated corresponding supertypes also reused one inference branch, which rejected `extend` interface implementation cases that official Cangjie accepts.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all` on `extend_overload_builtin_op1.cj` reports built-in operator overload diagnostics for `Int64` operators, unary `-`, `RETURN_TYPE_INCOMPATIBLE` for `Int64.==`, and `sema_extend_member_cannot_shadow` for `Bool.==`; `extend_overload_builtin_op5.cj` reports only `sema_return_type_incompatible` on the whole `extend Int32 <: Add<Int32> {}` declaration plus missing `main`; `extend_overload_builtin_op6.cj` reports only `sema_static_variable_use_generic_parameter` on `T` and does not reject `v2 != v3`. A focused `Bool`/`Unit` equality probe confirmed official Cangjie reports both built-in overload and `sema_extend_member_cannot_shadow` for `==`/`!=`, while `Bool &&` and `Bool ||` are parser-invalid overload forms rather than shadow-check cases. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckDecl.cpp` `TypeCheckerImpl::CheckOperatorOverloadFunc`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/BuiltInInheritanceHelper.cpp` `StructInheritanceChecker::IsBuiltInOperatorFuncInExtend`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` `CheckExtendMemberValid`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/BuiltInOperatorUtil.cpp`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOperatorModifierChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/resolution.common/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/resolve/calls/inference/components/ConstraintInjector.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/inference/ConeConstraintSystemUtilContext.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/cfir-cones/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/types/BuiltinPrimitiveOperators.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirOperatorDeclarationChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirExtendExtraChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirDeclarationDiagnosticSources.kt`, `common/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/type/AbstractTypeChecker.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/inference/ConeConstraintSystemUtilContext.kt`, generated diagnostic surfaces for `OPERATOR_OVERLOAD_BUILT_IN_UNARY_OPERATOR`, `OPERATOR_OVERLOAD_BUILT_IN_BINARY_OPERATOR`, and `RETURN_TYPE_INCOMPATIBLE`, plus fixture correction in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_overload_builtin_op1.cj`.
- repair principle: keep primitive operator classification in the shared built-in primitive operator model, keep extend shadow/implementation checks in the shared declaration checkers, and let the shared type checker fork duplicated-supertype subtype checks instead of adding fixture-local exceptions.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp1`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp5`, `testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp6`, and the same three methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated`.
- fixture correction: `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_overload_builtin_op1.cj` now marks built-in overload diagnostics on the full `public` token, following this repository's Diagnostic Range Policy rather than cjc's narrow anchor.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp5' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp6' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp5' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendOverloadBuiltinOp6' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all three focused PSI and non-PSI primitive built-in operator methods passed.

## Extend inherited-interface members do not shadow extend implementations

- problem type: extend members that implement an inherited interface member, including a default interface function, must not be reported as `EXTEND_MEMBER_CANNOT_SHADOW`.
- root cause: `CfirExtendExtraChecker` filtered only abstract interface requirements from the target shadow scope. When the extended target inherited an interface default function, the checker treated that inherited interface member as an existing target member and reported a false shadow diagnostic on the extend implementation.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\Extend\autobox_match4.cj` produced no diagnostics for `extend Int64 <: I { public func f() ... }`. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` `StructInheritanceChecker::CheckSameNameInheritanceInfo` skips shadow/member-kind checks when `parent.isInheritedInterface` is true before calling `CheckExtendMemberValid`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirExtendExtraChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the rule in the shared extend shadow checker by excluding all inherited interface members, not just abstract interface requirements, so PSI and light-tree paths use the same official inherited-interface semantics.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch3`, `testAutoboxMatch4`, `testAutoboxTryCatch1`, `testAutoboxTryCatch2`, and the same four methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxTryCatch1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxTryCatch1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxTryCatch2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxTryCatch2' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all four focused PSI and non-PSI methods passed after the inherited-interface shadow fix and the try/catch DCE fix below.

## Try/catch DCE unused diagnostics for no-throw try bodies

- problem type: a catch block with no executable throwing predecessor must not make a local variable count as used and must not report an unused expression from the unreachable catch result.
- root cause: `CfirUnusedExpressionChecker` used syntax traversal for both local variable usage and unused-expression reporting. It traversed every catch body even when the try body was locally known not to throw, so `y` inside the unreachable catch both suppressed `UNUSED_VARIABLE` on the declaration and produced `UNUSED_EXPRESSION` on the catch result.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cfir-probes\autobox_try_catch1_clean.cj` reported only `chir_dce_unused_variable` on `y` at line 16 column 9. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/CHIR/Transformation/DeadCodeElimination.cpp` reports unused locals through `ReportUnusedLocalVariable`, reports unused expressions only when `blockUsed` is true, and defines `CheckUselessBlock` as a non-entry block with no predecessors and no `UNREACHABLE` attribute.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirUnusedCheckerBase.kt`, which is the local architecture counterpart for declaration-driven unused-expression traversal; official Cangjie CHIR DCE supplies the Cangjie-specific reachability/user semantics.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirUnusedExpressionChecker.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the no-throw catch reachability decision in the shared unused checker and make both variable-use collection and expression reporting consume the same conservative `mayThrowException` predicate, rather than editing the `autobox_try_catch1` fixture.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxTryCatch1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxTryCatch1`; guard fixture `testAutoboxTryCatch2` stayed passing in both PSI and non-PSI suites.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch3' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxMatch4' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxTryCatch1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxTryCatch1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxTryCatch2' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testAutoboxTryCatch2' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; all focused PSI and non-PSI match/try-catch methods passed.

## Extend invalid inherited-interface filtering

- problem type: invalid interface supertypes must report their declaration-site diagnostics and must not enter extend orphan/duplicate/interface-closure indexes.
- root cause: `CfirExtendIndexStore` treated every resolved class-like supertype as an inherited interface candidate while building extend direct-interface and interface-closure indexes. Error types, class supertypes inherited by interfaces, and interfaces whose own supertype list was semantically invalid could therefore leak into extend semantic indexes and produce secondary extend diagnostics after the official root error was already known.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cfir-probes\extend_invalid_type02_clean.cj` reported only `sema_undeclared_type_name` on `T` and `sema_interface_inherit_non_interface` on `Strbase`; it did not report extend orphan, duplicate, or cycle diagnostics. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExtend.cpp` `CheckExtendDecl`, which removes invalid inherited types, and `TypeManager::GetAllSuperTys`, which returns no supertypes for incorrect types.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirSupertypesChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/services/CfirExtendIndexStore.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_invalid_type02.cj`.
- repair principle: keep invalid inherited-interface filtering in the shared extend semantic index, so orphan, duplicate, closure, default-member, PSI, and light-tree consumers all see the same official valid-interface graph.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInvalidType02`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInvalidType02`.
- fixture correction: `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/Extend/extend_invalid_type02.cj` now places `INTERFACE_CANNOT_INHERIT_CLASS` on the interface declaration name and keeps `UNDECLARED_TYPE_NAME` on `T`, matching this repository's full-token diagnostic range policy and official root diagnostics.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInvalidType02' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendInvalidType02' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; focused PSI and non-PSI invalid-type methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=0 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=0 errors=0`.

## Extend composition callable-reference ambiguity suppression

- problem type: in `f ~> g`, a function-reference ambiguity on the composition right operand is a flow-expression candidate-selection detail and must not be reported as a second ordinary `AMBIGUOUS_USE` after the left operand/root diagnostic has already failed.
- root cause: CFIR desugared `f ~> g` to the core intrinsic `composition(f, g)` but the error-node collector still treated the second argument's `CfirErrorNamedReference(ConeAmbiguityError)` as an ordinary qualified access. The callable-reference no-match mapper also returned a single diagnostic, so it could not express "handled by the argument's existing ambiguity" without falling back to outer no-match diagnostics.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cfir-probes\extend_function_conflict_clean.cj` reported one `sema_ambiguous_use` at the first `i.foo` in `let i2 = i.foo ~> i.foo`, plus the two shadow diagnostics; it did not report a no-match-ref diagnostic or a second ambiguous-use diagnostic on the right operand. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/BinaryExpr.cpp` `ChkFlowExpr`, which marks the composition right expression as `isInFlowExpr`, and through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp`, where `isInFlowExpr` puts overloaded function references into a target/flow context instead of ordinary reference ambiguity reporting.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/ResolutionStages.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CheckCallableReferenceExpectedType.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/semantics/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/ResolutionDiagnostic.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/collectors/components/ErrorNodeDiagnosticCollectorComponent.kt`.
- repair principle: keep callable-reference no-match classification in the shared cone-diagnostic mapper and keep flow-expression right-operand ambiguity suppression in the shared error-reference collector, so every `composition(f, g)` desugaring follows official flow-reference semantics without fixture-local checks.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFunctionConflict`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFunctionConflict`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFunctionConflict' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated.testExtendFunctionConflict' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; focused PSI and non-PSI function-conflict methods passed.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.Extend.ExtendGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed non-PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=0 errors=0` and PSI `ExtendGenerated` `tests=164 failures=0 errors=0`.

## Class implicit super constructor visibility across packages

- problem type: an implicit `super()` call must fail with `NO_MATCH_FUNCTION_DECLARATION_FOR_CALL` when the direct parent has only no-parameter constructors that are not visible at the derived constructor use site.
- root cause: `CfirConstructorDelegationChecker` checked only whether the direct parent declared a no-parameter constructor. It did not apply constructor use-site visibility, so a cross-package default `internal init()` was accepted as a valid implicit `super()` target.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Parse/ParserModifierRules.cpp` `ParserImpl::GetModifierAttr` returns `PUBLIC` only for package specs, `PRIVATE` only for import specs, and `INTERNAL` for ordinary declarations with no modifier; this fixture is therefore default `internal`, not private. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckUtil.cpp` `IsLegalAccess` checks `Attribute::INTERNAL` through package relation visibility. The probe command `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all --import-path tmp\cfir-probes\call-private-ctor-out-current tmp\cfir-probes\call_private_ctor_main.cj` reported two `sema_no_match_function_declaration_for_call` diagnostics for function call `super` at lines 4 and 7.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirStatusResolver.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/FirCallResolver.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConstructorDelegationChecker.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/transformers/CfirStatusResolveProcessor.kt`.
- repair principle: keep implicit constructor-delegation validation in the shared constructor checker and force constructor candidate collection to see resolved status before visibility-dependent selection, instead of changing default visibility or patching one fixture.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.call_private_ctor_in_other_package.CallPrivateCtorInOtherPackageGenerated.testMain`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.call_private_ctor_in_other_package.CallPrivateCtorInOtherPackageGenerated.testMain`.
- guard fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.protected.ProtectedGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.protected.ProtectedGenerated`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassPrivateInit`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassPrivateInit`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.call_private_ctor_in_other_package.CallPrivateCtorInOtherPackageGenerated.testMain' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.call_private_ctor_in_other_package.CallPrivateCtorInOtherPackageGenerated.testMain' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace --no-build-cache --rerun-tasks`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML from the full regression later showed non-PSI `CallPrivateCtorInOtherPackageGenerated` `tests=2 failures=0 errors=0` and PSI `CallPrivateCtorInOtherPackageGenerated` `tests=2 failures=0 errors=0`.
- guard verification commands: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.protected.ProtectedGenerated' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.protected.ProtectedGenerated' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`; `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassPrivateInit' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassPrivateInit' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- guard verification outcome: both guard commands finished `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: failed with remaining unrelated LLT failures: `8755 tests completed, 3375 failed, 310 skipped`. The largest remaining failing suites by XML are `GenericsGenerated`, `FunctionGenerated`, `ClassGenerated`, `EnumGenerated`, and `CallGenerated`; the repaired `CallPrivateCtorInOtherPackageGenerated` suites stayed green.

## Class static/redef override diagnostics

- problem type: class members that mix `static`, `redef`, and inherited non-static functions must follow official ordering: static/non-static name conflict suppresses ordinary missing-redef target diagnostics, instance `redef` reports both invalid modifier and no-needed-redef diagnostics, and static shadow of a non-open instance function also reports cannot-override.
- root cause: `CfirOverrideChecker` searched only direct override candidates that already matched static-ness, so a `static redef` function whose only inherited match was non-static fell through to `NOTHING_TO_OVERRIDE`. `CfirModifierChecker` also reported `NOTHING_TO_OVERRIDE` on the `redef` token source, but that diagnostic factory requires a named declaration source and crashes the PSI path. `class_redef_4.cj` missed the official cannot-override diagnostic.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cfir-probes\class_redef_2_clean.cj` reports only `sema_static_and_non_static_member_cannot_have_same_name` at line 8 column 5. `class_redef_3_clean.cj` reports `sema_redef_modify_static_func` on `redef` and `sema_func_no_override_or_redefine_modifier` on the declaration. `class_redef_4_clean.cj` reports `sema_static_and_non_static_member_cannot_have_same_name` and `sema_cannot_override` on `foo`. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` `CheckSameNameInheritanceInfo` and `CheckInheritanceAttributes`, plus `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckDecl.cpp` `TypeCheckerImpl::CheckFuncDecl`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirOverrideChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirModifierChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirOverrideChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`, and `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/class_redef_4.cj`.
- repair principle: keep source-modifier legality in the modifier checker, inherited override target lookup in the override checker, and static/non-static/cannot-override inheritance diagnostics in the shared inheritance checker, so PSI and light-tree paths follow the same official inherited-member semantics.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef2`, `testClassRedef3`, `testClassRedef4`, and the same three methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated`.

## Class generic ideal literal inference through primitive extend upper bound

- problem type: an implicit generic function type argument inferred from an ideal integer literal must satisfy an interface upper bound when exactly one concrete integer primitive reaches that interface through `extend`.
- root cause: CFIR did not expose `ConeIdeal*` literal/operator constructors to the shared type approximator, so `ResultTypeResolver` could not approximate `IdealInt` with its declared upper bound. The final call-completion writer then defaulted the inferred type argument to `Int64`, and the generic upper-bound checker reported `Int64 <: I` as false even though official `IdealInt <: I` is accepted via `extend Int32 <: I`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\class\class_redef_12.cj` reported only two `chir_dce_unused_variable` warnings and no errors. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeManager.cpp` `IsPrimitiveSubtype` treats `TYPE_IDEAL_INT` as a subtype if any concrete integer primitive is a subtype of the root, and `GetAllExtendsByTy` expands ideal primitives through `GetIdealTypesByKind`; `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckUtil.cpp` defines that concrete ideal-int expansion order. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/LocalTypeArgumentSynthesis.cpp` orders non-ideal constraints before ideal constraints so ideal literals can be restricted by surrounding bounds.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/resolution.common/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/resolve/calls/inference/components/ResultTypeResolver.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/resolution.common/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/types/AbstractTypeApproximator.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/resolution.common/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/types/TypeApproximatorConfiguration.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/types/ConeInferenceContext.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirCallCompletionResultsWriterTransformer.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/types/ConeTypeContext.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/types/ConeInferenceContext.kt`, `cfir/cfir-cones/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/types/ConeIntegerLiteralType.kt`.
- repair principle: keep ideal-literal approximation in the shared type-system/inference context and let the existing upper-bound checker consume the corrected inferred type argument, rather than suppressing `GENERIC_TYPE_ARGUMENT_NOT_MATCH_CONSTRAINT` at the fixture or diagnostic layer.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef12`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef12`.
- guard fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef11`, `testClassRedef15`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef11`, `testClassRedef15`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallGenericUpper06`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallGenericUpper06`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef12' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef12' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: outer command timed out at 240 seconds while Gradle was still running; after the wrapper exited, XML showed both focused PSI and non-PSI `testClassRedef12` methods passed with `failures=0 errors=0`.
- guard verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef11' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef11' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef12' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef12' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef15' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef15' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallGenericUpper06' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.call.CallGenerated.testCallGenericUpper06' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testInstantiateExtendRearrange02' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.generics.GenericsGenerated.testInstantiateExtendRearrange02' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## Class invalid type-parameter upper bounds suppress member-access cascades

- problem type: when a type parameter has an illegal non-class/interface upper bound, member/operator access through that type parameter must not report secondary ambiguity or no-match diagnostics after `UPPER_BOUND_MUST_BE_CLASS_OR_INTERFACE`.
- root cause: CFIR reported the illegal upper bound in `CfirTypeParameterBoundsChecker`, but resolver/type-scope code still collected member scopes from the illegal primitive bound `T <: Int64`. That let `x + 1` resolve against primitive operator candidates and produced a cascade `AMBIGUOUS_FUNCTION_CALL` in `class_redef_14`.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cfir-probes\class_redef_14_clean.cj` reported only `sema_upper_bound_must_be_class_or_interface` at `T` and no `+` ambiguity. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/AST/Types.h` `GenericsTy::isUpperBoundLegal`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/PreCheck.cpp` upper-bound sanity checks, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp` `GetMemberAccessExposedTarget`, which returns without member lookup when the generic upper bound is not legal.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`, especially `containsErrorTypeForSuppressingAmbiguityError`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirTypeParameterBoundsChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/types/TypeUtils.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/calls/CfirReceivers.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassStaticScope.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`.
- repair principle: derive the official invalid-upper-bound state in the shared type-system layer, prevent invalid type-parameter bounds from contributing receiver/static scopes, and mark accesses through such receivers as already-covered cascades instead of suppressing one fixture diagnostic.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef14`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef14`.
- guard fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef15`, `testClassRedef19`, `testClassRedef21`, and the same three methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated`.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef14' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef14' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef15' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef15' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef19' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef19' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef21' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassRedef21' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## Constructor delegation non-first calls still report target argument diagnostics

- problem type: a non-first `this(...)` / `super(...)` constructor delegation call must report the illegal placement diagnostic and must still continue through the ordinary constructor target/argument-count checks.
- root cause: `CfirConstructorDelegationChecker` reported `ILLEGAL_PLACE_OF_CALLING_THIS_OR_SUPER` for non-first delegation calls and then stopped. This skipped the same target argument diagnostics that first-position delegation calls already receive, so `invalid_init_call_1.cj` lost `NO_VALUE_FOR_PARAMETER` on the illegal `this()` call.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all tmp\cfir-probes\invalid_init_call_1_clean.cj` reported `sema_illegal_place_of_calling_this_or_super` on `this`, `sema_wrong_number_of_arguments` on `()`, and the two immutable-assignment diagnostics. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeChecker.cpp` `TypeCheckerImpl::CheckCallsInConstructor`, which walks all constructor calls and reports illegal placement without aborting other call checking, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` `TypeCheckerImpl::CheckCallKind`, whose comment states that a call of `this()` is treated as a normal declared function call.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirCommonConstructorDelegationIssuesChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToFirDiagnostic.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/FirArgumentsToParametersMapper.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirConstructorDelegationChecker.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/invalid_init_call_1.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/invalid_super_call.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/constructor/primaryConstructor/primaryConstructor2.cj`.
- repair principle: keep non-first explicit delegation calls in the shared constructor-delegation checker, report their placement error first, then reuse the same explicit `this`/`super` target checker used for legal first-position delegation calls rather than adding a fixture-local `NO_VALUE_FOR_PARAMETER` path.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testInvalidInitCall1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testInvalidInitCall1`.
- guard fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassPrivateInit`, `testInterfaceInitInvalid1`, `testInvalidSuperCall2`, and the same three methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated`.
- fixture correction: `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/invalid_init_call_1.cj` now expects `ILLEGAL_PLACE_OF_CALLING_THIS_OR_SUPER` on the full `this` keyword and `NO_VALUE_FOR_PARAMETER` on `()`, matching official semantics plus this repository's full-token range policy. `invalid_super_call.cj` and `primaryConstructor2.cj` were also updated from one-character `s`/`t` ranges to full `super`/`this` keyword ranges for the same range policy; their remaining semantic mismatches are separate existing problem families.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testInvalidInitCall1' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testInvalidInitCall1' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## This/super call and bare super diagnostic classification

- problem type: `this(...)` / `super(...)` outside constructors, top-level `this`/`super` use, bare `super` in class bodies, and illegal-position `super()` receiver chains must use the official diagnostic classes without field-initializer or unresolved-reference cascades.
- root cause: CFIR treated all illegal `this(...)` / `super(...)` constructor-delegation-shaped calls as the older generic `ILLEGAL_THIS_OR_SUPER_CALL`, reported field-initializer `this`/`super` diagnostics on constructor-call callees, did not report bare `super` through the super-reference checker, and gave illegal-position `super()` calls `Unit`/generic error types that either lost nominal receiver lookup (`NOT_MEMBER_OF`) or let downstream member access produce cascades.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --error-count-limit all` on `tmp\cfir-probes\invalid_super_call_clean.cj`, `invalid_super_call_2_clean.cj`, `interface_init_invalid_1_clean.cj`, `class_super_alone_invalid_1_clean.cj`, and `class8_clean.cj` reported `sema_this_super_use_error_outside_class`, `sema_invalid_this_call_outside_ctor`, `sema_illegal_super_alone`, `sema_illegal_place_of_calling_this_or_super`, and `sema_not_member_of` in the corresponding source positions. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` `CheckThisOrSuper`, `CheckUsageOfSuper`, and `CheckThisOrSuperInInitializer`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` `CheckRefConstructor`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/NameReferenceExpr.cpp` `DiagMemberAccessNotFound`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/psi2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/builder/PsiRawFirBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/light-tree2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/lightTree/LightTreeRawFirExpressionBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/expression/FirSuperReferenceChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/checkers-component-generator/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/checkers/generator/diagnostics/CfirDiagnosticsList.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrors.kt`, `cfir/checkers/gen/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirNonSuppressibleErrorNames.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/CfirErrorsDefaultMessages.kt`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testFixtures/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/tests/golden/DiagnosticNameMapper.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirConstructorDelegationCallChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirIllegalSuperReferenceChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirFieldVariableThisOrSuperInitializerChecker.kt`, and `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`.
- repair principle: keep the official classification split at the shared expression checker and super-reference checker boundaries, while resolve preserves either the nominal receiver type for illegal-position constructor calls or an unreported `SuperNotAllowed` error type for outside-constructor calls so downstream diagnostics follow the same root cause instead of fixture-local expectations.

## Abstract class inherited static interface member diagnostics

- problem type: an abstract class that inherits an unimplemented static interface member must report `INHERIT_ABSTRACT_CLASS_STATIC_UNIMPLEMENT_FUNC`, and class/typealias nominal static calls to that unimplemented member must report `INTERFACE_CALL_WITH_UNIMPLEMENTED_CALL`.
- root cause: `CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.checkAbstractClassStaticUnimplemented` returned early for abstract classes, only inspected direct `CfirClass` parents, and therefore skipped direct interfaces and inherited use-site members. No expression checker reported the official nominal-qualifier access diagnostic for resolved abstract static callable targets.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/InheritanceChecker/StructInheritanceChecker.cpp` `DiagnoseForUnimplementedInterfaces` reports `sema_inherit_abstract_class_static_unimplement_func` when the current declaration is an abstract class and an inherited static abstract member remains unimplemented. `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` `IsNamespaceMemberAccessLegal` reports `sema_interface_call_with_unimplemented_call` when a nominal target accesses an abstract member. Focused `cjc` probes for `class_static_member_access_invalid02` and `class_static_member_access_invalid05` reported both diagnostics on `A` and `test`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirNotImplementedOverrideChecker.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/checkers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/analysis/checkers/declaration/FirImplementationMismatchChecker.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/declaration/CfirInheritanceDeepChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirInterfaceCallWithUnimplementedCallChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/CommonExpressionCheckers.kt`.
- repair principle: use the existing use-site member scope and inherited-member model to find unimplemented static abstract requirements for abstract classes, and place nominal static-call diagnostics in a qualified-access checker so class names, interfaces, and typealiases share one path.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid02`, `testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid05`, and the same two methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated`.

## Static qualifier generic substitution inconsistency

- problem type: inherited or extend-provided static member calls through a generic nominal qualifier must report `GENERIC_TYPE_INCONSISTENT` when the qualifier owner type parameter is inferred to multiple concrete types.
- root cause: CFIR already substituted inherited/extend static member signatures to the use-site qualifier type and added the resulting owner fresh-variable constraints, but call resolution classified the resulting contradiction as an ordinary candidate/inference failure and did not preserve the official base-expression diagnostic owner.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckCall.cpp` `CheckAndGetMappingForTypeDecl` computes the mapping from `baseExpr`/type declaration generics to the target static-member owner and reports `sema_generic_type_inconsistent` on `baseExpr` when one generic maps to multiple concrete types; `CheckCandidateConstrains` calls it for member-access calls whose base type declaration differs from the static member owner.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CreateFreshTypeVariableSubstitutorStage.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/stages/CheckArguments.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/diagnostic/ConeDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirCallResolver.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: classify the contradiction at the shared call-resolution result boundary by inspecting static qualifier owner fresh-variable lower/equality constraints, and map the dedicated Cone diagnostic to the qualifier receiver source so inherited class members and static extend members share the same path.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid03`, `testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid04`, and the same two methods in `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated`.
- guard fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid02`, `testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid05`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer01`, `testTypealiasPartialInfer03`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.interface.interface_default_impl.InterfaceDefaultImplGenerated.testInterfaceStaticImplWithGeneric12`, and the corresponding PSI methods.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid03' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid03' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid04' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid04' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: outer command timed out after 244 seconds, but the Gradle Java worker completed; XML showed both PSI and non-PSI generated classes ran `testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid03` and `testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid04` with `failures=0 errors=0`.
- guard verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid02' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid02' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid05' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid05' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer01' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer01' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer03' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt._typealias.TypealiasGenerated.testTypealiasPartialInfer03' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.interface.interface_default_impl.InterfaceDefaultImplGenerated.testInterfaceStaticImplWithGeneric12' --tests 'org.cangnova.cangjie.cfir.analysis.tests.CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.interface.interface_default_impl.InterfaceDefaultImplGenerated.testInterfaceStaticImplWithGeneric12' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## Object receiver access to static member diagnostics

- problem type: object receivers such as `B().a` / `super.i` / `super.foo()` must report `OBJECT_CANNOT_ACCESS_STATIC_MEMBER`, while type/class qualifiers for static members and static extend calls must remain valid static lookup bases.
- root cause: CFIR did not separate runtime expression receivers from class/type/builtin qualifier receivers in the dispatch-receiver stage, so static members could either be checked as ordinary receiver-applicable members or static qualifier calls could be rejected before owner/static lookup semantics completed.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` `FilterAndGetTargetsOfObjAccess` filters static members out of object access and reports `sema_object_cannot_access_static_member`; `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagnosticSema.def` defines that diagnostic. Focused `cjc` probes for `B().a`, `super.i`, and `super.foo()` reported the object-static diagnostic, while `CString.test()` and bare static calls inside a static extend context compiled.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirStaticScope.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/calls/tower/TowerLevels.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckDispatchReceiver.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/tower/TowerLevelHandler.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, and range-only fixture corrections in `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/class/interface_this_invalid_1.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/binary/binary_error_report_02.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/call/variadic_err_static.cj`, `cfir/analysis-tests/testData/llt/enum/enum16_memberFunc.cj`.
- repair principle: classify object-static misuse at the shared dispatch-receiver applicability stage, and treat class/type/builtin qualifiers as static lookup bases rather than runtime dispatch receivers.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid`, `testInterfaceThisInvalid1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.call.CallGenerated.testVariadicErrStatic`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.binary.BinaryGenerated.testBinaryErrorReport02`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.enum.EnumGenerated.testEnum16MemberFunc`, and the corresponding PSI methods.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceThisInvalid1' --tests '*CallGenerated.testVariadicErrStatic' --tests '*BinaryGenerated.testBinaryErrorReport02' --tests '*EnumGenerated.testEnum16MemberFunc' --tests '*EnumGenerated.testEnum29' --tests '*ExtendEnumPropertyGenerated.testExtendEnumProperty1' --tests '*ArrayGenerated.testArrayAccess4' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; the full focused PSI/non-PSI slice completed with no failures.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: outer command timed out during worker shutdown, then XML completed. Aggregate XML showed `8756 tests`, `3296 failures`, `0 errors`; the covered object-static methods listed above had no failure nodes in PSI or non-PSI XML.

## Type qualifier access to non-static member diagnostics

- problem type: class/type/builtin qualifiers used to access instance members, including enum instance functions, private instance members, static-extend instance properties, and `Array<Bool>[3]` get/set, must report `ILLEGAL_ACCESS_NON_STATIC_MEMBER` instead of `REF_NOT_BE_TYPE`, `NOT_MEMBER_OF`, visibility/private diagnostics, or no diagnostic.
- root cause: static qualifier lookup filtered or bypassed instance candidates before the official non-static-member classification point; subscript receivers were resolved as ordinary expressions and array fast paths bypassed the synthetic `get`/`set` call resolver, so type qualifiers either produced classifier-as-expression cascades or skipped the non-static diagnostic entirely.
- official Cangjie evidence: `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckReference.cpp` `FilterAndCheckTargetsOfNameAccess` erases non-static targets from type-name access and reports `sema_illegal_access_non_static_member` when the filtered set is empty; `CheckAccessLegalityOfMemberAccess` / `CheckStaticMemberAccessLegality` apply the same rule to member access; `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagnosticSema.def` defines the text `'%s' is non-static member, cannot access by type name`. Focused `cjc` probes showed enum `ET.foo1()`, struct `A.a` / private `A.b` / `A.m()` / private `A.l()`, extend enum properties `Data.n` / `Data.m`, and `Array<Bool>[3]` all use this diagnostic; `A + A` instead reports binary/type-expression diagnostics, not the non-static-member diagnostic.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/providers/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/scopes/impl/FirStaticScope.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/psi2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/builder/PsiRawFirBuilder.kt`, `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/raw-fir/light-tree2fir/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/lightTree/converter/LightTreeRawFirExpressionBuilder.kt`, and `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/semantics/ResolutionDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/semantics/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/diagnostic/ConeDiagnostic.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/ResolveUtils.kt`, `cfir/providers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/scopes/impl/CfirClassStaticScope.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckDispatchReceiver.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/calls/stages/CfirCheckExtensionReceiver.kt`, `cfir/resolve/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/resolve/body/CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirClassifierAsExpressionChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`, diagnostic generated files and message/name mappings, plus range fixture corrections in `enum16_memberFunc.cj`, `enum29.cj`, `extend_enum_property1.cj`, and `array_access4.cj`.
- repair principle: keep type qualifiers as receiver-structure inputs to the shared call resolver, expose same-name instance candidates only when static filtering leaves no static target, and report the official non-static-member diagnostic from the central candidate-diagnostic mapping layer.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.enum.EnumGenerated.testEnum16MemberFunc`, `testEnum29`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.Extend.property.extend_enum_property.ExtendEnumPropertyGenerated.testExtendEnumProperty1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.array.ArrayGenerated.testArrayAccess4`, and the corresponding PSI methods.
- guard fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt._class.ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid`, `testInterfaceThisInvalid1`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.call.CallGenerated.testVariadicErrStatic`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.binary.BinaryGenerated.testBinaryErrorReport02`, and the corresponding PSI methods; `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.lookup.multi_files_private_00.MultiFilesPrivate00Generated.testMain` and its PSI method also passed after static-scope narrowing.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*ClassGenerated.testClassStaticMemberAccessInvalid' --tests '*ClassGenerated.testInterfaceThisInvalid1' --tests '*CallGenerated.testVariadicErrStatic' --tests '*BinaryGenerated.testBinaryErrorReport02' --tests '*EnumGenerated.testEnum16MemberFunc' --tests '*EnumGenerated.testEnum29' --tests '*ExtendEnumPropertyGenerated.testExtendEnumProperty1' --tests '*ArrayGenerated.testArrayAccess4' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`

## Binary operator diagnostics with classifier operands and hidden operator candidates

- problem type: binary expressions such as `A + A` must keep both operand `REF_NOT_BE_TYPE` diagnostics and the outer `INVALID_BINARY_OPERATOR`, while hidden/private operator candidates such as `A() - A()` must be reported as invalid binary operator calls instead of no-match or visibility/private diagnostics.
- root cause: `CfirClassifierAsExpressionChecker` skipped all `CfirFunctionCall` nodes and treated the left operand of a plain operator call as an outer qualified-access receiver, so `A + A` lost the left `REF_NOT_BE_TYPE` and outer binary diagnostic. Separately, `ConeHiddenCandidateError` mapped hidden operator candidates through the ordinary no-match path, which misclassified private operator syntax.
- official Cangjie evidence: focused `cjc --diagnostic-format json` probe with `struct A { public operator func +(q: A) {}; private operator func -(s: A) {} }` showed `A + A` reports `sema_invalid_binary_expr` on `+` plus `sema_ref_not_be_type` on both operands, `A() + A()` compiles, and `A() - A()` reports `sema_invalid_binary_expr` on `-`. Official implementation was traced through `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/BinaryExpr.cpp` `SynBinaryExpr` / `DiagnoseForBinaryExpr`, `external/cangjie_compiler/src/Sema/TypeCheckExpr/Diags.cpp` `DiagInvalidBinaryExpr`, and `external/cangjie_compiler/include/cangjie/Basic/DiagRefactor/DiagnosticSema.def` `sema_invalid_binary_expr`.
- Kotlin counterpart files consulted: `external/kotlin/compiler/fir/resolve/src/org/jetbrains/kotlin/fir/resolve/transformers/body/resolve/FirExpressionsResolveTransformer.kt`, where operator syntax is represented as synthetic function calls with `FirFunctionCallOrigin.Operator`.
- CFIR owner files changed: `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/checkers/expression/CfirClassifierAsExpressionChecker.kt`, `cfir/checkers/src/org/cangnova/cangjie/cfir/analysis/diagnostics/coneDiagnosticToCfirDiagnostic.kt`.
- repair principle: preserve classifier operands as runtime expression uses for plain operator calls and classify hidden operator candidates at the shared candidate-diagnostic mapping layer, rather than adding fixture-local binary-expression checks.
- fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.record.RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl`, `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.record.RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl`.
- guard fixtures covered: `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.binary.BinaryGenerated.testBinaryErrorReport02`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.enum.EnumGenerated.testEnum16MemberFunc`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.array.ArrayGenerated.testArrayAccess4`, `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.lookup.multi_files_private_00.MultiFilesPrivate00Generated.testMain`, and the corresponding PSI methods.
- verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed both `CfirAnalysisLLTTest.llt.record.RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl` and `CfirAnalysisLLTPsiTest.llt.record.RecordGenerated.testRecordAccessControl` passed with `tests=1 failures=0 errors=0`.
- guard verification command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --tests '*BinaryGenerated.testBinaryErrorReport02' --tests '*EnumGenerated.testEnum16MemberFunc' --tests '*ArrayGenerated.testArrayAccess4' --tests '*MultiFilesPrivate00Generated.testMain' --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- guard verification outcome: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL`; XML showed all eight PSI/non-PSI guard methods passed with `failures=0 errors=0`.
- regression command: `.\gradlew.bat :cfir:analysis-tests:test --no-daemon --max-workers=1 --console=plain --stacktrace`
- regression outcome: `BUILD FAILED` with remaining unrelated LLT failures. Aggregate XML showed `8756 tests`, `3294 failures`, `0 errors`, `311 skipped`; the covered `testRecordAccessControl` and guard methods remained passed in both PSI and non-PSI XML.

## Unit-return match branch numeric conversion expected-type mismatch

- problem type: numeric conversion expressions inside nested `match` branches in an explicit `Unit` function body must keep their synthesized primitive result type and must not report `TYPE_MISMATCH` solely because the outer function body supplies `Unit` as expected type.
- root cause: `CfirExpressionsResolveTransformer.transformTypeConversion` treated `ResolutionMode.WithExpectedType` as a local hard requirement and wrapped a valid numeric conversion result in `ConeTypeMismatchError` when the synthesized primitive type was not a subtype of the outer expected type. In `box_in_multi_matchcase.cj`, the explicit `Unit` return type reaches the nested `match` branch as expected type, so `Int64(v)` was converted into an erroneous expression before the function-body checker could apply the explicit-`Unit` rule.
- official Cangjie evidence: `cjc --diagnostic-format json --output %TEMP%\codex-box-in-multi-matchcase cfir\analysis-tests\testData\llt\box\box_in_multi_matchcase.cj` exited `0` with no diagnostics. A guard probe on `box_in_generic.cj` exited `0` with only `chir_dce_unused_expression`, confirming that non-`Unit` return target typing remains real Cangjie semantics and must not be removed from function body resolution.
